# LOOPC_MODEL_AS_MF1 (W-DYNA, generated 2026-11-27 15:19Z from W-DYNML bundles; W-DYNML owns the models, code/th_dynml)

SPEC (bundle files are written by dml_algo_hookfit.py). Book AS_MF1 = cell Lb0e6dfa3_R0_F40_80. Two ONE-PERIOD bundles = the last purged walk-forward fit of each refit step (this is the model that runs for any trade after the last period: the hook reuses the last model; the full-history bundles hold 12 (s13) / 39 (s4) periods and are on server C under /mnt/l2/l2vast/_dynml/algo/hook/loopc_Lb0e6dfa3_R0_F40_80_s<STEP>.json.gz).

How to read: `base64 -d | gunzip` gives JSON {"tag","step","feats":[18 names],"safety_mult":1.5,"periods":[{"wk0","wk1","t0","t1","fit_end","n_fit_signals","models":[ {"init":0.0,"trees":[{"f","t","l","r","d","m","v"} x60]} x4 ]}]}. Models in order: take-all, tighten, part, trail. Tree node arrays are indexed by node number, root = 0; f = split feature index into feats (-1 = leaf), t = threshold, l / r = child node numbers, d = default-left flag, m = missing type (0 None, 1 Zero, 2 NaN), v = leaf value. Evaluation: see LOOPC_SPEC.md section f (pure loop, no library). Training: LightGBM LGBMRegressor(n_estimators=60, learning_rate=0.1, num_leaves=15, min_child_samples=300, subsample=0.7, subsample_freq=1, colsample_bytree=0.7, reg_lambda=10.0, max_bin=63), target = clip(advantage over hold, -3, 3) in R0 units, rows = 1-minute states of past trades of the book, at most 150,000 rows, only trades that ENDED before fit_end (purge), at most 104 weeks back, at least 150 distinct trades.
